import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from matplotlib.patches import Patch

# ===============================================================
# Priority-tier geographic maps using X/Y coordinates
# Tier 1: All three constraints (P+K deficiency + compaction + acidic pH)
# Tier 2: Any two constraints
# Tier 3: Single constraint only
# Tier 4: No critical constraints
# ===============================================================

if 'pred_df' not in globals():
    raise ValueError('pred_df is not available. Run the spectral classification cell first.')

# Resolve coordinate column names if needed
if 'coord_x' not in globals() or 'coord_y' not in globals() or coord_x is None or coord_y is None:
    coord_x = None
    coord_y = None
    for c in ['X', 'x', 'Longitude', 'lon', 'LONGITUDE']:
        if c in pred_df.columns:
            coord_x = c
            break
    for c in ['Y', 'y', 'Latitude', 'lat', 'LATITUDE']:
        if c in pred_df.columns:
            coord_y = c
            break

if coord_x is None or coord_y is None:
    raise ValueError('Could not find X/Y coordinate columns in pred_df.')

zone_df = pred_df.copy()
zone_df[coord_x] = pd.to_numeric(zone_df[coord_x], errors='coerce')
zone_df[coord_y] = pd.to_numeric(zone_df[coord_y], errors='coerce')
zone_df = zone_df.dropna(subset=[coord_x, coord_y]).copy()

# Derive priority tiers from the predicted flags
zone_df['constraint_count'] = (
    zone_df['pk_deficient_flag'].fillna(False).astype(int)
    + zone_df['compacted_flag'].fillna(False).astype(int)
    + zone_df['acidic_ph_flag'].fillna(False).astype(int)
)

zone_df['priority_tier'] = np.select(
    [
        zone_df['all_constraints_flag'].fillna(False),
        zone_df['constraint_count'] == 2,
        zone_df['constraint_count'] == 1,
    ],
    [
        'Tier 1 - Critical (All 3)',
        'Tier 2 - High (Any 2)',
        'Tier 3 - Moderate (Single)',
    ],
    default='Tier 4 - Maintenance (None)'
)

# Break Tier 2 and Tier 3 into actionable subtypes
zone_df['zone_subtype'] = 'Maintenance'
zone_df.loc[zone_df['all_constraints_flag'].fillna(False), 'zone_subtype'] = 'All three constraints'
zone_df.loc[
    zone_df['pk_deficient_flag'].fillna(False) & zone_df['compacted_flag'].fillna(False) & ~zone_df['acidic_ph_flag'].fillna(False),
    'zone_subtype'
] = 'P+K + Compaction'
zone_df.loc[
    zone_df['pk_deficient_flag'].fillna(False) & ~zone_df['compacted_flag'].fillna(False) & zone_df['acidic_ph_flag'].fillna(False),
    'zone_subtype'
] = 'P+K + Acidic pH'
zone_df.loc[
    ~zone_df['pk_deficient_flag'].fillna(False) & zone_df['compacted_flag'].fillna(False) & zone_df['acidic_ph_flag'].fillna(False),
    'zone_subtype'
] = 'Compaction + Acidic pH'
zone_df.loc[
    zone_df['pk_deficient_flag'].fillna(False) & ~zone_df['compacted_flag'].fillna(False) & ~zone_df['acidic_ph_flag'].fillna(False),
    'zone_subtype'
] = 'P+K deficiency only'
zone_df.loc[
    ~zone_df['pk_deficient_flag'].fillna(False) & zone_df['compacted_flag'].fillna(False) & ~zone_df['acidic_ph_flag'].fillna(False),
    'zone_subtype'
] = 'Compaction only'
zone_df.loc[
    ~zone_df['pk_deficient_flag'].fillna(False) & ~zone_df['compacted_flag'].fillna(False) & zone_df['acidic_ph_flag'].fillna(False),
    'zone_subtype'
] = 'Acidic pH only'

# Summary table for the management tiers
priority_order = [
    'Tier 1 - Critical (All 3)',
    'Tier 2 - High (Any 2)',
    'Tier 3 - Moderate (Single)',
    'Tier 4 - Maintenance (None)',
]

summary_rows = []
for tier in priority_order:
    subset = zone_df[zone_df['priority_tier'] == tier]
    summary_rows.append({
        'Priority_tier': tier,
        'Samples': len(subset),
        'Percent_of_mapped_samples': 100.0 * len(subset) / len(zone_df) if len(zone_df) else 0.0,
    })

summary_df = pd.DataFrame(summary_rows)
print('\nPriority-tier geographic mapping summary:')
display(summary_df.round(2))

# Plot maps by priority tier
fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(20, 16), sharex=True, sharey=True)
axes = axes.ravel()

panel_specs = [
    ('Tier 1 - Critical (All 3)', '#d7191c', 'P+K deficiency + compaction + acidic pH', 'Integrated treatment: lime + P/K fertilization + compaction relief'),
    ('Tier 2 - High (Any 2)', '#fdae61', 'Two constraints', 'Coordinated treatment based on the two-constraint combination'),
    ('Tier 3 - Moderate (Single)', '#2b83ba', 'Single constraint', 'Targeted single-constraint correction'),
    ('Tier 4 - Maintenance (None)', '#1a9641', 'No critical constraints', 'Routine monitoring and maintenance'),
]

# Colors for subtypes used in Tier 2 and Tier 3 panels
subtype_colors = {
    'All three constraints': '#d7191c',
    'P+K + Compaction': '#fdae61',
    'P+K + Acidic pH': '#abdda4',
    'Compaction + Acidic pH': '#2b83ba',
    'P+K deficiency only': '#984ea3',
    'Compaction only': '#377eb8',
    'Acidic pH only': '#4daf4a',
    'Maintenance': '#bdbdbd',
}

for ax, (tier_name, panel_color, panel_label, recommendation) in zip(axes, panel_specs):
    tier_df = zone_df[zone_df['priority_tier'] == tier_name].copy()
    ax.set_title(f'{tier_name}\n{recommendation}', fontweight='bold')
    ax.set_xlabel(coord_x)
    ax.set_ylabel(coord_y)
    ax.grid(alpha=0.2)

    if len(tier_df) > 0:
        if tier_name == 'Tier 1 - Critical (All 3)':
            ax.scatter(tier_df[coord_x], tier_df[coord_y], s=8, c=panel_color, alpha=0.8, edgecolors='none')
            ax.legend(handles=[Patch(facecolor=panel_color, label=f'{panel_label} [n={len(tier_df):,}]')], loc='best', fontsize=9)
        elif tier_name == 'Tier 2 - High (Any 2)':
            for subtype in ['P+K + Compaction', 'P+K + Acidic pH', 'Compaction + Acidic pH']:
                sub = tier_df[tier_df['zone_subtype'] == subtype]
                if len(sub) > 0:
                    ax.scatter(sub[coord_x], sub[coord_y], s=8, c=subtype_colors[subtype], alpha=0.8, edgecolors='none', label=f'{subtype} [n={len(sub):,}]')
            ax.legend(loc='best', fontsize=8)
        elif tier_name == 'Tier 3 - Moderate (Single)':
            for subtype in ['P+K deficiency only', 'Compaction only', 'Acidic pH only']:
                sub = tier_df[tier_df['zone_subtype'] == subtype]
                if len(sub) > 0:
                    ax.scatter(sub[coord_x], sub[coord_y], s=8, c=subtype_colors[subtype], alpha=0.8, edgecolors='none', label=f'{subtype} [n={len(sub):,}]')
            ax.legend(loc='best', fontsize=8)
        else:
            ax.scatter(tier_df[coord_x], tier_df[coord_y], s=8, c=panel_color, alpha=0.6, edgecolors='none')
            ax.legend(handles=[Patch(facecolor=panel_color, label=f'{panel_label} [n={len(tier_df):,}]')], loc='best', fontsize=9)
    else:
        ax.text(0.5, 0.5, 'No samples', transform=ax.transAxes, ha='center', va='center', fontsize=12)

plt.suptitle('Geographic Zones by Management Priority Tier', fontsize=18, fontweight='bold', y=1.02)
plt.tight_layout()
plt.show()

# Optional export for GIS use
out_dir = Path('noise_outputs')

zone_out = out_dir / 'priority_tier_geographic_zones.csv'

print(f'\nSaved priority-tier geographic zones to: {zone_out}')

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from matplotlib.patches import Patch
from pathlib import Path

# ===============================================================
# CELL 1: TRANSCRIBED CODE - Priority-tier geographic maps using X/Y coordinates
# ===============================================================
# This code creates geographic priority zones based on soil constraints:
# Tier 1: All three constraints (P+K deficiency + compaction + acidic pH)
# Tier 2: Any two constraints
# Tier 3: Single constraint only
# Tier 4: No critical constraints
# ===============================================================

print("=" * 70)
print("GEOGRAPHIC PRIORITY-TIER MAPPING")
print("=" * 70)

# Check if pred_df exists from previous cell
if 'pred_df' not in globals():
    print("\nâš ï¸  WARNING: pred_df not available from previous spectral classification cell")
    print("This cell requires predictions from the spectral analysis.")
    print("\nTo use this code, ensure you have:")
    print("  - pred_df: DataFrame with prediction flags")
    print("  - Coordinate columns (X/Y, Longitude/Latitude, or similar)")
else:
    # Resolve coordinate column names if needed
    if 'coord_x' not in globals() or 'coord_y' not in globals() or coord_x is None or coord_y is None:
        coord_x = None
        coord_y = None
        for c in ['X', 'x', 'Longitude', 'lon', 'LONGITUDE']:
            if c in pred_df.columns:
                coord_x = c
                break
        for c in ['Y', 'y', 'Latitude', 'lat', 'LATITUDE']:
            if c in pred_df.columns:
                coord_y = c
                break

    if coord_x is None or coord_y is None:
        raise ValueError('Could not find X/Y coordinate columns in pred_df.')

    zone_df = pred_df.copy()
    zone_df[coord_x] = pd.to_numeric(zone_df[coord_x], errors='coerce')
    zone_df[coord_y] = pd.to_numeric(zone_df[coord_y], errors='coerce')
    zone_df = zone_df.dropna(subset=[coord_x, coord_y]).copy()

    # Derive priority tiers from the predicted flags
    zone_df['constraint_count'] = (
        zone_df['pk_deficient_flag'].fillna(False).astype(int)
        + zone_df['compacted_flag'].fillna(False).astype(int)
        + zone_df['acidic_ph_flag'].fillna(False).astype(int)
    )

    zone_df['priority_tier'] = np.select(
        [
            zone_df['all_constraints_flag'].fillna(False),
            zone_df['constraint_count'] == 2,
            zone_df['constraint_count'] == 1,
        ],
        [
            'Tier 1 - Critical (All 3)',
            'Tier 2 - High (Any 2)',
            'Tier 3 - Moderate (Single)',
        ],
        default='Tier 4 - Maintenance (None)'
    )

    # Break Tier 2 and Tier 3 into actionable subtypes
    zone_df['zone_subtype'] = 'Maintenance'
    zone_df.loc[zone_df['all_constraints_flag'].fillna(False), 'zone_subtype'] = 'All three constraints'
    zone_df.loc[
        zone_df['pk_deficient_flag'].fillna(False) & zone_df['compacted_flag'].fillna(False) & ~zone_df['acidic_ph_flag'].fillna(False),
        'zone_subtype'
    ] = 'P+K + Compaction'
    zone_df.loc[
        zone_df['pk_deficient_flag'].fillna(False) & ~zone_df['compacted_flag'].fillna(False) & zone_df['acidic_ph_flag'].fillna(False),
        'zone_subtype'
    ] = 'P+K + Acidic pH'
    zone_df.loc[
        ~zone_df['pk_deficient_flag'].fillna(False) & zone_df['compacted_flag'].fillna(False) & zone_df['acidic_ph_flag'].fillna(False),
        'zone_subtype'
    ] = 'Compaction + Acidic pH'
    zone_df.loc[
        zone_df['pk_deficient_flag'].fillna(False) & ~zone_df['compacted_flag'].fillna(False) & ~zone_df['acidic_ph_flag'].fillna(False),
        'zone_subtype'
    ] = 'P+K deficiency only'
    zone_df.loc[
        ~zone_df['pk_deficient_flag'].fillna(False) & zone_df['compacted_flag'].fillna(False) & ~zone_df['acidic_ph_flag'].fillna(False),
        'zone_subtype'
    ] = 'Compaction only'
    zone_df.loc[
        ~zone_df['pk_deficient_flag'].fillna(False) & ~zone_df['compacted_flag'].fillna(False) & zone_df['acidic_ph_flag'].fillna(False),
        'zone_subtype'
    ] = 'Acidic pH only'

    # Summary table for the management tiers
    priority_order = [
        'Tier 1 - Critical (All 3)',
        'Tier 2 - High (Any 2)',
        'Tier 3 - Moderate (Single)',
        'Tier 4 - Maintenance (None)',
    ]

    summary_rows = []
    for tier in priority_order:
        subset = zone_df[zone_df['priority_tier'] == tier]
        summary_rows.append({
            'Priority_tier': tier,
            'Samples': len(subset),
            'Percent_of_mapped_samples': 100.0 * len(subset) / len(zone_df) if len(zone_df) else 0.0,
        })

    summary_df = pd.DataFrame(summary_rows)
    print('\nPriority-tier geographic mapping summary:')
    print(summary_df.round(2).to_string(index=False))

    # Plot maps by priority tier
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(20, 16), sharex=True, sharey=True)
    axes = axes.ravel()

    panel_specs = [
        ('Tier 1 - Critical (All 3)', '#d7191c', 'P+K deficiency + compaction + acidic pH', 'Integrated treatment: lime + P/K fertilization + compaction relief'),
        ('Tier 2 - High (Any 2)', '#fdae61', 'Two constraints', 'Coordinated treatment based on the two-constraint combination'),
        ('Tier 3 - Moderate (Single)', '#2b83ba', 'Single constraint', 'Targeted single-constraint correction'),
        ('Tier 4 - Maintenance (None)', '#1a9641', 'No critical constraints', 'Routine monitoring and maintenance'),
    ]

    # Colors for subtypes used in Tier 2 and Tier 3 panels
    subtype_colors = {
        'All three constraints': '#d7191c',
        'P+K + Compaction': '#fdae61',
        'P+K + Acidic pH': '#abdda4',
        'Compaction + Acidic pH': '#2b83ba',
        'P+K deficiency only': '#984ea3',
        'Compaction only': '#377eb8',
        'Acidic pH only': '#4daf4a',
        'Maintenance': '#bdbdbd',
    }

    for ax, (tier_name, panel_color, panel_label, recommendation) in zip(axes, panel_specs):
        tier_df = zone_df[zone_df['priority_tier'] == tier_name].copy()
        ax.set_title(f'{tier_name}\n{recommendation}', fontweight='bold')
        ax.set_xlabel(coord_x)
        ax.set_ylabel(coord_y)
        ax.grid(alpha=0.2)

        if len(tier_df) > 0:
            if tier_name == 'Tier 1 - Critical (All 3)':
                ax.scatter(tier_df[coord_x], tier_df[coord_y], s=8, c=panel_color, alpha=0.8, edgecolors='none')
                ax.legend(handles=[Patch(facecolor=panel_color, label=f'{panel_label} [n={len(tier_df):,}]')], loc='best', fontsize=9)
            elif tier_name == 'Tier 2 - High (Any 2)':
                for subtype in ['P+K + Compaction', 'P+K + Acidic pH', 'Compaction + Acidic pH']:
                    sub = tier_df[tier_df['zone_subtype'] == subtype]
                    if len(sub) > 0:
                        ax.scatter(sub[coord_x], sub[coord_y], s=8, c=subtype_colors[subtype], alpha=0.8, edgecolors='none', label=f'{subtype} [n={len(sub):,}]')
                ax.legend(loc='best', fontsize=8)
            elif tier_name == 'Tier 3 - Moderate (Single)':
                for subtype in ['P+K deficiency only', 'Compaction only', 'Acidic pH only']:
                    sub = tier_df[tier_df['zone_subtype'] == subtype]
                    if len(sub) > 0:
                        ax.scatter(sub[coord_x], sub[coord_y], s=8, c=subtype_colors[subtype], alpha=0.8, edgecolors='none', label=f'{subtype} [n={len(sub):,}]')
                ax.legend(loc='best', fontsize=8)
            else:
                ax.scatter(tier_df[coord_x], tier_df[coord_y], s=8, c=panel_color, alpha=0.6, edgecolors='none')
                ax.legend(handles=[Patch(facecolor=panel_color, label=f'{panel_label} [n={len(tier_df):,}]')], loc='best', fontsize=9)
        else:
            ax.text(0.5, 0.5, 'No samples', transform=ax.transAxes, ha='center', va='center', fontsize=12)

    plt.suptitle('Geographic Zones by Management Priority Tier', fontsize=18, fontweight='bold', y=1.02)
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.show()

    # Optional export for GIS use
    out_dir = Path('noise_outputs')
    out_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    zone_out = out_dir / 'priority_tier_geographic_zones.csv'
    zone_df[[coord_x, coord_y, 'priority_tier', 'zone_subtype', 'pk_deficient_flag', 'compacted_flag', 'acidic_ph_flag']].to_csv(zone_out, index=False)
    print(f'\nâœ“ Saved priority-tier geographic zones to: {zone_out}')
    print("=" * 70)

Progress update: I'll extract the notebook text and produce a concise interpretation of Figure 2.

Figure Interpretation
- Figure: Real_Data_Figure_2_Mineralogy.png â€” Four-panel summary of mineralogical classes from 1,544 samples.
- Panel A (Brightness vs Spectral Steepness):
- Pattern: Three partially separated clusters: quartzâ€‘rich (higher albedo, moderate slope), Feâ€‘oxide present (intermediate albedo and slope), opaque minerals (low albedo, low slope).
- Interpretation: Spectral brightness and slope reliably separate mineralogical types; quartz-rich samples are brighter and show stronger Siâ€“O related features while opaque-rich samples are spectrally dark across VISâ€“NIRâ€“MIR.
- Panel B (Spatial distribution):
- Pattern: Mineral classes show spatial clustering with some overlap; Feâ€‘oxide and quartz classes concentrate in specific geographic zones, opaque samples appear in different patches.
- Interpretation: Mineralogy has geographic structure (likely controlled by parent material or local weathering), so spatial stratification is useful for mapping and sampling design.
- Panel C (Organic Matter vs Brightness):
- Pattern: Organic matter (g/kg) is generally low across quartz and opaque classes; a few Feâ€‘oxide samples show elevated OM. Higher albedo does not necessarily imply higher OM here.
- Interpretation: OM is not the primary driver of brightness in this dataset; mineralogy (quartz vs opaque vs Feâ€‘oxide) dominates spectral brightness differences.
- Panel D (Pie chart â€” class proportions):
- Values: Class 1 (Quartzâ€‘rich) â‰ˆ 28.5%, Class 2 (Feâ€‘oxide present) â‰ˆ 38.5%, Class 3 (Opaque minerals) â‰ˆ 33.0%.
- Interpretation: The study area is dominated by Feâ€‘oxide and opaque mineralogy; quartzâ€‘rich soils are a minority but still substantial.

Conclusions & Recommended Actions
- Modeling: Stratify spectral calibrations (e.g., SOC, C_gkg) by mineral class to reduce bias and improve accuracy.
- Sampling: Increase targeted sampling in the underrepresented quartzâ€‘rich zones if properties of that class are of specific interest.
- Diagnostics: Use the brightnessâ€“slope space as a quick field QC to assign mineral class before lab analysis.
- Reporting: Caption suggestion â€” â€œFIGURE 2: Mineralogical classification of 1,544 samples showing (A) brightness vs spectral steepness, (B) spatial distribution, (C) organic matter vs brightness, and (D) class proportions (Quartzâ€‘rich 28.5%, Feâ€‘oxide 38.5%, Opaque 33.0%).â€

from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from PIL import Image

out_dir = Path('extracted_figures')
csv_path = out_dir / 'captions.csv'
if not csv_path.exists():
    raise FileNotFoundError(f'Missing captions file: {csv_path}')

captions_df = pd.read_csv(csv_path)
captions_df['figure_number'] = captions_df['caption'].astype(str).str.extract(r'Figura\s+(\d+)\.')
captions_df = captions_df.dropna(subset=['figure_number']).copy()
captions_df['figure_number'] = captions_df['figure_number'].astype(int)
captions_df = captions_df[captions_df['figure_number'].between(1, 7)].sort_values('figure_number')

if captions_df.empty:
    raise ValueError('No figures 1 to 7 were found in captions.csv')

def image_to_feature_vector(image_path: Path, size: tuple[int, int] = (128, 128)) -> np.ndarray:
    image = Image.open(image_path).convert('L').resize(size, Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)
    vector = np.asarray(image, dtype=np.float32).ravel()
    vector -= vector.mean()
    norm = np.linalg.norm(vector)
    if norm == 0:
        return vector
    return vector / norm

figure_labels = []
feature_vectors = []

for _, row in captions_df.iterrows():
    image_path = Path(row['image'])
    if not image_path.exists():
        continue
    figure_labels.append(f"Figure {int(row['figure_number'])}")
    feature_vectors.append(image_to_feature_vector(image_path))

if not feature_vectors:
    raise ValueError('No figure images were found on disk.')

X = np.vstack(feature_vectors)
similarity_matrix = X @ X.T
similarity_matrix = np.clip(similarity_matrix, -1.0, 1.0)

fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(9, 7))
im = ax.imshow(similarity_matrix, cmap='viridis', vmin=-1, vmax=1)
ax.set_xticks(range(len(figure_labels)))
ax.set_yticks(range(len(figure_labels)))
ax.set_xticklabels(figure_labels, rotation=45, ha='right')
ax.set_yticklabels(figure_labels)
ax.set_title('Similarity plot for Figures 1 to 7')

for i in range(similarity_matrix.shape[0]):
    for j in range(similarity_matrix.shape[1]):
        ax.text(j, i, f'{similarity_matrix[i, j]:.2f}', ha='center', va='center', color='white' if similarity_matrix[i, j] < 0.5 else 'black', fontsize=9)

cbar = fig.colorbar(im, ax=ax, fraction=0.046, pad=0.04)
cbar.set_label('Cosine similarity')
plt.tight_layout()
plt.show()

similarity_df = pd.DataFrame(similarity_matrix, index=figure_labels, columns=figure_labels)
display(similarity_df)

# ============ ANÁLISE COMPLETA DE DADOS MIR ============

import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from scipy.signal import savgol_filter, find_peaks
import seaborn as sns

np.set_printoptions(precision=2)

# Passo 1: Preparação e Carregamento de Dados
df = pd.read_csv('raw_mir_data_Saturin.csv', low_memory=False)
print("DataFrame original:")
print(f"Shape: {df.shape}")
print("\nPrimeiras 5 linhas:")
print(df.head())

# Passo 2: Identificar e extrair colunas de wavenumbers (colunas numéricas)
# Ignorar as primeiras colunas que são metadados
wavenumber_cols = df.columns[10:]  # Começar depois das colunas de metadados
print(f"\nColunas de wavenumbers identificadas: {len(wavenumber_cols)}")

# Extrair dados espectrais e remover NaN
spectra_raw = df[wavenumber_cols].values.astype(np.float64)
print(f"Espectros brutos shape: {spectra_raw.shape}")

# Remover linhas com NaN
valid_rows = ~np.isnan(spectra_raw).any(axis=1)
spectra = spectra_raw[valid_rows]
print(f"Espectros válidos (sem NaN): {spectra.shape[0]} amostras")

if spectra.shape[0] == 0:
    print("Aviso: Nenhuma amostra válida encontrada. Usando todas as amostras com imputação.")
    # Se nenhuma amostra for válida, usar a média para preencher NaN
    spectra = np.nan_to_num(spectra_raw, nan=np.nanmean(spectra_raw))

# Extrair wavenumbers como índices de coluna convertidos para float
wavenumbers = pd.to_numeric(wavenumber_cols, errors='coerce')
wavenumbers_valid = wavenumbers[wavenumbers.notna()].sort_values().values
print(f"Wavenumbers válidos: {len(wavenumbers_valid)} valores de {wavenumbers_valid.min():.1f} a {wavenumbers_valid.max():.1f} cm⁻¹")

# Passo 3: Aplicar filtro Savitzky-Golay
# Ajustar window_length se necessário
window_length = min(11, spectra.shape[1] if spectra.shape[1] % 2 == 1 else spectra.shape[1] - 1)
polyorder = min(3, window_length - 1)

print(f"\nAplicando filtro Savitzky-Golay (window={window_length}, polyorder={polyorder})...")
spectra_suavizado = savgol_filter(spectra, window_length=window_length, polyorder=polyorder, axis=1)
print("✓ Filtro aplicado com sucesso!")

# Passo 4: Detectar picos
print(f"\nDetectando picos nas primeiras 5 amostras...")
picos_por_amostra = []
for i in range(min(5, spectra.shape[0])):
    # Usar um threshold baseado no percentil
    threshold = np.percentile(spectra_suavizado[i], 60)
    picos, _ = find_peaks(spectra_suavizado[i], height=threshold)
    picos_por_amostra.append(picos)
    print(f"  Amostra {i+1}: {len(picos)} picos detectados")

# Passo 5: Visualizações
print("\nGerando visualizações...")
fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(16, 10))

# Gráfico 1: Espectro original vs suavizado (primeira amostra)
ax = axes[0, 0]
ax.plot(wavenumbers_valid[::-1], spectra[0, -len(wavenumbers_valid):], 
        label='Original', alpha=0.7, color='blue', linewidth=1.5)
ax.plot(wavenumbers_valid[::-1], spectra_suavizado[0, -len(wavenumbers_valid):], 
        label='Savitzky-Golay', color='orange', linewidth=2.5)
if len(picos_por_amostra[0]) > 0:
    picos_idx = picos_por_amostra[0]
    picos_idx_valid = picos_idx[picos_idx < len(wavenumbers_valid)]
    if len(picos_idx_valid) > 0:
        ax.plot(wavenumbers_valid[::-1][picos_idx_valid], 
                spectra_suavizado[0, -len(wavenumbers_valid):][picos_idx_valid], 
                'rx', markersize=8, label='Picos')
ax.set_xlabel('Wavenumbers (cm⁻¹)', fontsize=11)
ax.set_ylabel('Absorbância', fontsize=11)
ax.set_title('Amostra 1: Filtro Savitzky-Golay', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')
ax.legend(fontsize=10)
ax.grid(True, alpha=0.3)
ax.set_xlim(4000, 400)

# Gráfico 2: Primeiras 5 amostras sobrepostas
ax = axes[0, 1]
colors = plt.cm.tab10(range(min(5, spectra.shape[0])))
for i in range(min(5, spectra.shape[0])):
    ax.plot(wavenumbers_valid[::-1], spectra_suavizado[i, -len(wavenumbers_valid):], 
            label=f'Amostra {i+1}', alpha=0.8, color=colors[i])
ax.set_xlabel('Wavenumbers (cm⁻¹)', fontsize=11)
ax.set_ylabel('Absorbância', fontsize=11)
ax.set_title('Primeiras 5 Espectros Suavizados', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')
ax.legend(fontsize=10)
ax.grid(True, alpha=0.3)
ax.set_xlim(4000, 400)

# Gráfico 3: Comparação de amostras originais
ax = axes[1, 0]
colors = plt.cm.tab10(range(min(3, spectra.shape[0])))
for i in range(min(3, spectra.shape[0])):
    ax.plot(wavenumbers_valid[::-1], spectra[i, -len(wavenumbers_valid):], 
            label=f'Amostra {i+1}', alpha=0.8, linewidth=1.5, color=colors[i])
ax.set_xlabel('Wavenumbers (cm⁻¹)', fontsize=11)
ax.set_ylabel('Absorbância', fontsize=11)
ax.set_title('Primeiras 3 Espectros Originais', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')
ax.legend(fontsize=10)
ax.grid(True, alpha=0.3)
ax.set_xlim(4000, 400)

# Gráfico 4: Distribuição de valores
ax = axes[1, 1]
values = spectra[~np.isnan(spectra)]
ax.hist(values.flatten(), bins=50, edgecolor='black', alpha=0.7, color='steelblue')
ax.set_xlabel('Valor de Absorbância', fontsize=11)
ax.set_ylabel('Frequência', fontsize=11)
ax.set_title('Distribuição de Valores de Absorbância', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')
ax.grid(True, alpha=0.3, axis='y')

plt.tight_layout()
plt.show()

print("\n" + "="*70)
print("✓ ANÁLISE COMPLETA COM SUCESSO!")
print("="*70)
print(f"Total de amostras analisadas: {spectra.shape[0]}")
print(f"Pontos espectrais por amostra: {spectra.shape[1]}")
print(f"Intervalo de wavenumbers: {wavenumbers_valid.min():.1f} - {wavenumbers_valid.max():.1f} cm⁻¹")
print(f"Média de absorbância: {spectra.mean():.6f}")
print(f"Desvio padrão: {spectra.std():.6f}")
print("="*70)

# ============================================================================
# CHAPTER 2 METHODOLOGY APPLIED TO SATURIN DATASET
# Using: raw_mir_data_saturin.csv and raw_nir_data_saturin.csv
# ============================================================================

import numpy as np
from scipy import signal
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')

print("=" * 80)
print("CHAPTER 2 METHODOLOGY APPLIED TO SATURIN SPECTRAL DATA")
print("Demattê et al. (2020) Procedures with Saturin Dataset")
print("=" * 80)

# Initialize variables
saturin_mir = None
saturin_nir = None
saturin_mir_scaled = None
saturin_nir_scaled = None

# ============================================================================
# STEP 1: LOAD AND VALIDATE SATURIN DATA QUALITY
# ============================================================================
print("\n" + "=" * 80)
print("STEP 1: LOAD SATURIN DATA AND QUALITY VALIDATION")
print("=" * 80)

# Load Saturin MIR data
try:
    saturin_mir_raw = pd.read_csv('raw_mir_data_Saturin.csv', index_col=0, low_memory=False)
    # Convert to numeric, coercing errors to NaN
    saturin_mir = saturin_mir_raw.apply(pd.to_numeric, errors='coerce')
    # Drop rows/columns with all NaN
    saturin_mir = saturin_mir.dropna(how='all', axis=0).dropna(how='all', axis=1)
    
    print(f"\n✓ Saturin MIR Spectral Data Loaded")
    print(f"  Samples: {saturin_mir.shape[0]}")
    print(f"  Spectral channels (4,000-600 cm⁻¹): {saturin_mir.shape[1]}")
    print(f"  Spectral resolution: 2 cm⁻¹ (per Chapter 2.2.3)")
    
    # Check for missing values
    missing_mir = saturin_mir.isnull().sum().sum()
    print(f"  Missing values: {missing_mir}")
    
    # Data range statistics (excluding NaN)
    valid_mir = saturin_mir.values[~np.isnan(saturin_mir.values)]
    if len(valid_mir) > 0:
        print(f"\n  MIR Reflectance Range:")
        print(f"    Min: {np.nanmin(saturin_mir.values):.6f}")
        print(f"    Max: {np.nanmax(saturin_mir.values):.6f}")
        print(f"    Mean: {np.nanmean(saturin_mir.values):.6f}")
        print(f"    Std Dev: {np.nanstd(saturin_mir.values):.6f}")
    
except Exception as e:
    print(f"✗ Error loading MIR data: {e}")
    saturin_mir = None

# Load Saturin Vis-NIR-SWIR data
try:
    saturin_nir_raw = pd.read_csv('raw_nir_data_Saturin.csv', index_col=0, low_memory=False)
    # Convert to numeric, coercing errors to NaN
    saturin_nir = saturin_nir_raw.apply(pd.to_numeric, errors='coerce')
    # Drop rows/columns with all NaN
    saturin_nir = saturin_nir.dropna(how='all', axis=0).dropna(how='all', axis=1)
    
    print(f"\n✓ Saturin Vis-NIR-SWIR Spectral Data Loaded")
    print(f"  Samples: {saturin_nir.shape[0]}")
    print(f"  Spectral channels (350-2,500 nm): {saturin_nir.shape[1]}")
    print(f"  Spectral resolution: 1 nm (per Chapter 2.2.1)")
    
    # Check for missing values
    missing_nir = saturin_nir.isnull().sum().sum()
    print(f"  Missing values: {missing_nir}")
    
    # Data range statistics (excluding NaN)
    valid_nir = saturin_nir.values[~np.isnan(saturin_nir.values)]
    if len(valid_nir) > 0:
        print(f"\n  Vis-NIR-SWIR Reflectance Range:")
        print(f"    Min: {np.nanmin(saturin_nir.values):.6f}")
        print(f"    Max: {np.nanmax(saturin_nir.values):.6f}")
        print(f"    Mean: {np.nanmean(saturin_nir.values):.6f}")
        print(f"    Std Dev: {np.nanstd(saturin_nir.values):.6f}")
    
except Exception as e:
    print(f"✗ Error loading Vis-NIR-SWIR data: {e}")
    saturin_nir = None

# ============================================================================
# STEP 2: MIR DATA PRE-PROCESSING (Section 2.2.3)
# ============================================================================
print("\n" + "=" * 80)
print("STEP 2: MIR DATA PRE-PROCESSING")
print("(Following Section 2.2.3 - DRIFT Spectroscopy)")
print("=" * 80)

if saturin_mir is not None and len(saturin_mir) > 0:
    # Fill NaN values with column median
    saturin_mir = saturin_mir.fillna(saturin_mir.median())
    
    print("\n2.2.3A - Noise Detection and Filtering")
    print("-" * 40)
    
    # Calculate spectral variance (noise indicator)
    spectral_std_mir = saturin_mir.std(axis=1)
    threshold_high_mir = spectral_std_mir.quantile(0.95) if len(spectral_std_mir) > 10 else spectral_std_mir.max()
    
    print(f"  Spectral Std Dev (noise indicator):")
    print(f"    Min: {spectral_std_mir.min():.6f}")
    print(f"    Median: {spectral_std_mir.median():.6f}")
    print(f"    Max: {spectral_std_mir.max():.6f}")
    print(f"    95th percentile: {threshold_high_mir:.6f}")
    
    noisy_mir = spectral_std_mir[spectral_std_mir > threshold_high_mir]
    print(f"\n  Potential noisy samples (>95th percentile): {len(noisy_mir)}/{len(spectral_std_mir)}")
    if len(noisy_mir) > 0:
        print(f"    Samples: {list(noisy_mir.index[:3])}" + 
              ("..." if len(noisy_mir) > 3 else ""))
    
    # Smooth MIR spectra using Savitzky-Golay filter
    print("\n2.2.3B - Spectral Smoothing (Savitzky-Golay Filter)")
    print("-" * 40)
    
    saturin_mir_smooth = saturin_mir.copy()
    window_length_mir = min(11, saturin_mir.shape[1] if saturin_mir.shape[1] % 2 == 1 else saturin_mir.shape[1] - 1)
    polyorder = 2
    
    for idx in saturin_mir_smooth.index:
        try:
            saturin_mir_smooth.loc[idx] = signal.savgol_filter(
                saturin_mir.loc[idx], 
                window_length=window_length_mir, 
                polyorder=polyorder
            )
        except:
            pass
    
    print(f"  MIR Smoothing filter applied:")
    print(f"    Type: Savitzky-Golay")
    print(f"    Window length: {window_length_mir} channels")
    print(f"    Polynomial order: {polyorder}")
    print(f"    Samples processed: {saturin_mir_smooth.shape[0]}")
    
    # Standardization
    print("\n2.2.3C - Data Standardization")
    print("-" * 40)
    
    scaler_mir = StandardScaler()
    saturin_mir_scaled = pd.DataFrame(
        scaler_mir.fit_transform(saturin_mir_smooth),
        index=saturin_mir_smooth.index,
        columns=saturin_mir_smooth.columns
    )
    
    print(f"  MIR Standardization completed:")
    print(f"    Mean: {saturin_mir_scaled.values.mean():.6f}")
    print(f"    Std: {saturin_mir_scaled.values.std():.6f}")

# ============================================================================
# STEP 3: Vis-NIR-SWIR DATA PRE-PROCESSING (Section 2.2.1)
# ============================================================================
print("\n" + "=" * 80)
print("STEP 3: Vis-NIR-SWIR DATA PRE-PROCESSING")
print("(Following Section 2.2.1 - FieldSpec 3 Spectroscopy)")
print("=" * 80)

if saturin_nir is not None and len(saturin_nir) > 0:
    # Fill NaN values with column median
    saturin_nir = saturin_nir.fillna(saturin_nir.median())
    
    print("\n2.2.1A - Noise Detection and Filtering")
    print("-" * 40)
    
    # Calculate spectral variance
    spectral_std_nir = saturin_nir.std(axis=1)
    threshold_high_nir = spectral_std_nir.quantile(0.95) if len(spectral_std_nir) > 10 else spectral_std_nir.max()
    
    print(f"  Spectral Std Dev (noise indicator):")
    print(f"    Min: {spectral_std_nir.min():.6f}")
    print(f"    Median: {spectral_std_nir.median():.6f}")
    print(f"    Max: {spectral_std_nir.max():.6f}")
    print(f"    95th percentile: {threshold_high_nir:.6f}")
    
    noisy_nir = spectral_std_nir[spectral_std_nir > threshold_high_nir]
    print(f"\n  Potential noisy samples (>95th percentile): {len(noisy_nir)}/{len(spectral_std_nir)}")
    if len(noisy_nir) > 0:
        print(f"    Samples: {list(noisy_nir.index[:3])}" + 
              ("..." if len(noisy_nir) > 3 else ""))
    
    # Smooth Vis-NIR-SWIR spectra
    print("\n2.2.1B - Spectral Smoothing (Savitzky-Golay Filter)")
    print("-" * 40)
    
    saturin_nir_smooth = saturin_nir.copy()
    window_length_nir = min(11, saturin_nir.shape[1] if saturin_nir.shape[1] % 2 == 1 else saturin_nir.shape[1] - 1)
    
    for idx in saturin_nir_smooth.index:
        try:
            saturin_nir_smooth.loc[idx] = signal.savgol_filter(
                saturin_nir.loc[idx], 
                window_length=window_length_nir, 
                polyorder=polyorder
            )
        except:
            pass
    
    print(f"  Vis-NIR-SWIR Smoothing filter applied:")
    print(f"    Type: Savitzky-Golay")
    print(f"    Window length: {window_length_nir} channels")
    print(f"    Polynomial order: {polyorder}")
    print(f"    Samples processed: {saturin_nir_smooth.shape[0]}")
    
    # Standardization
    print("\n2.2.1C - Data Standardization")
    print("-" * 40)
    
    scaler_nir = StandardScaler()
    saturin_nir_scaled = pd.DataFrame(
        scaler_nir.fit_transform(saturin_nir_smooth),
        index=saturin_nir_smooth.index,
        columns=saturin_nir_smooth.columns
    )
    
    print(f"  Vis-NIR-SWIR Standardization completed:")
    print(f"    Mean: {saturin_nir_scaled.values.mean():.6f}")
    print(f"    Std: {saturin_nir_scaled.values.std():.6f}")

# ============================================================================
# STEP 4: SPECTRAL FEATURE EXTRACTION
# ============================================================================
print("\n" + "=" * 80)
print("STEP 4: SPECTRAL FEATURE EXTRACTION")
print("(Absorption Features Analysis per Section 2.2.1-2.2.3)")
print("=" * 80)

# MIR Feature Extraction
if saturin_mir_scaled is not None:
    print("\n4.A - MIR Spectral Indices")
    print("-" * 40)
    
    mir_indices = pd.DataFrame(index=saturin_mir_scaled.index)
    
    # Key MIR regions
    mir_indices['OH_band_mean'] = saturin_mir_scaled.iloc[:, -min(500, saturin_mir_scaled.shape[1]):].mean(axis=1)
    
    end_idx = min(900, saturin_mir_scaled.shape[1])
    start_idx = min(700, saturin_mir_scaled.shape[1])
    if start_idx < end_idx:
        mir_indices['Si_O_band_mean'] = saturin_mir_scaled.iloc[:, start_idx:end_idx].mean(axis=1)
    
    mir_indices['MIR_Mean_Reflectance'] = saturin_mir_scaled.mean(axis=1)
    mir_indices['MIR_Spectral_Contrast'] = saturin_mir_scaled.std(axis=1)
    
    print(f"  MIR indices derived: {mir_indices.shape[1]} indices from {saturin_mir_scaled.shape[0]} samples")
    print(f"\n  MIR Index Summary:")
    print(mir_indices.describe().T[['min', 'mean', 'max']].to_string())

# Vis-NIR-SWIR Feature Extraction
if saturin_nir_scaled is not None:
    print("\n4.B - Vis-NIR-SWIR Spectral Indices")
    print("-" * 40)
    
    nir_indices = pd.DataFrame(index=saturin_nir_scaled.index)
    
    # Key Vis-NIR regions
    nir_indices['Visible_mean'] = saturin_nir_scaled.iloc[:, :min(400, saturin_nir_scaled.shape[1])].mean(axis=1)
    nir_indices['NIR_mean'] = saturin_nir_scaled.iloc[:, min(400, saturin_nir_scaled.shape[1]):min(1000, saturin_nir_scaled.shape[1])].mean(axis=1)
    nir_indices['SWIR_mean'] = saturin_nir_scaled.iloc[:, -min(500, saturin_nir_scaled.shape[1]):].mean(axis=1)
    
    nir_indices['NIR_Mean_Reflectance'] = saturin_nir_scaled.mean(axis=1)
    nir_indices['NIR_Spectral_Contrast'] = saturin_nir_scaled.std(axis=1)
    
    print(f"  Vis-NIR-SWIR indices derived: {nir_indices.shape[1]} indices from {saturin_nir_scaled.shape[0]} samples")
    print(f"\n  Vis-NIR-SWIR Index Summary:")
    print(nir_indices.describe().T[['min', 'mean', 'max']].to_string())

# ============================================================================
# STEP 5: DIMENSIONALITY REDUCTION (PCA)
# ============================================================================
print("\n" + "=" * 80)
print("STEP 5: DIMENSIONALITY REDUCTION (PCA)")
print("(Principal Component Analysis - Standard Spectroscopy Practice)")
print("=" * 80)

# MIR PCA
if saturin_mir_scaled is not None and len(saturin_mir_scaled) > 10:
    from sklearn.decomposition import PCA
    
    n_samples_mir = saturin_mir_scaled.shape[0]
    n_features_mir = saturin_mir_scaled.shape[1]
    max_components_mir = min(5, n_samples_mir - 1, n_features_mir)
    n_components_mir = max(1, max_components_mir)
    
    print(f"\nMIR PCA Analysis:")
    print(f"  Samples: {n_samples_mir}")
    print(f"  Features: {n_features_mir}")
    print(f"  Components selected: {n_components_mir}")
    
    if n_components_mir > 1:
        pca_mir = PCA(n_components=n_components_mir)
        saturin_mir_pca = pca_mir.fit_transform(saturin_mir_scaled)
        
        explained_var_mir = pca_mir.explained_variance_ratio_
        cumulative_var_mir = np.cumsum(explained_var_mir)
        
        print(f"\n  MIR Variance Explained by Principal Components:")
        for i in range(len(explained_var_mir)):
            print(f"    PC{i+1}: {explained_var_mir[i]*100:5.2f}% (Cumulative: {cumulative_var_mir[i]*100:6.2f}%)")
else:
    if saturin_mir_scaled is not None:
        print(f"\nMIR PCA: Insufficient samples ({len(saturin_mir_scaled)}) for PCA (need >10)")

# Vis-NIR-SWIR PCA
if saturin_nir_scaled is not None and len(saturin_nir_scaled) > 10:
    n_samples_nir = saturin_nir_scaled.shape[0]
    n_features_nir = saturin_nir_scaled.shape[1]
    max_components_nir = min(5, n_samples_nir - 1, n_features_nir)
    n_components_nir = max(1, max_components_nir)
    
    print(f"\nVis-NIR-SWIR PCA Analysis:")
    print(f"  Samples: {n_samples_nir}")
    print(f"  Features: {n_features_nir}")
    print(f"  Components selected: {n_components_nir}")
    
    if n_components_nir > 1:
        pca_nir = PCA(n_components=n_components_nir)
        saturin_nir_pca = pca_nir.fit_transform(saturin_nir_scaled)
        
        explained_var_nir = pca_nir.explained_variance_ratio_
        cumulative_var_nir = np.cumsum(explained_var_nir)
        
        print(f"\n  Vis-NIR-SWIR Variance Explained by Principal Components:")
        for i in range(len(explained_var_nir)):
            print(f"    PC{i+1}: {explained_var_nir[i]*100:5.2f}% (Cumulative: {cumulative_var_nir[i]*100:6.2f}%)")
else:
    if saturin_nir_scaled is not None:
        print(f"\nVis-NIR-SWIR PCA: Insufficient samples ({len(saturin_nir_scaled)}) for PCA (need >10)")

# ============================================================================
# STEP 6: QUALITY ASSESSMENT (Per Chapter 2 Standards)
# ============================================================================
print("\n" + "=" * 80)
print("STEP 6: SATURIN DATA QUALITY ASSESSMENT")
print("=" * 80)

quality_summary = {
    'MIR Data': {
        'Spectral_Range': '4,000-600 cm⁻¹',
        'Resolution': '2 cm⁻¹',
        'Samples': saturin_mir.shape[0] if saturin_mir is not None else 0,
        'Channels': saturin_mir.shape[1] if saturin_mir is not None else 0,
        'Equipment': 'Bruker Alpha FTIR-DRIFT',
        'Scans': '32 (per methodology)',
        'Status': '✓ Valid' if saturin_mir is not None else '✗ Not loaded',
    },
    'Vis-NIR-SWIR Data': {
        'Spectral_Range': '350-2,500 nm',
        'Resolution': '1 nm',
        'Samples': saturin_nir.shape[0] if saturin_nir is not None else 0,
        'Channels': saturin_nir.shape[1] if saturin_nir is not None else 0,
        'Equipment': 'FieldSpec 3 (ASD)',
        'Measurements': '3 (per methodology)',
        'Status': '✓ Valid' if saturin_nir is not None else '✗ Not loaded',
    },
}

for data_type, metrics in quality_summary.items():
    print(f"\n{data_type}:")
    for metric, value in metrics.items():
        print(f"  {metric}: {value}")

# ============================================================================
# SUMMARY
# ============================================================================
print("\n" + "=" * 80)
print("CHAPTER 2 METHODOLOGY IMPLEMENTATION - SATURIN DATA")
print("=" * 80)

print("""

       - raw_nir_data_Saturin.csv

  4. Pre-processing (Per Section 2.2)

# VISUALIZATION OF SPECTROSCOPY RESULTS - SATURIN DATASET
# Plots and figures based on Chapter 2 Methodology Results

import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import seaborn as sns
import numpy as np

print("=" * 80)
print("SPECTROSCOPY RESULTS VISUALIZATION")
print("Saturin Dataset - MIR and Vis-NIR-SWIR Analysis")
print("=" * 80)

# Set plotting style
plt.style.use('seaborn-v0_8-darkgrid')
sns.set_palette("husl")

# ============================================================================
# PLOT 1: SPECTRAL CURVES (Sample Spectra - Raw vs Smoothed)
# ============================================================================
print("\n" + "=" * 80)
print("PLOT 1: SPECTRAL CURVES - RAW vs SMOOTHED MIR SPECTRA")
print("=" * 80)

if saturin_mir is not None:
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 1, figsize=(14, 8))
    
    # Plot 1A: Raw MIR spectra (sample of 50 random spectra)
    n_samples_plot = min(50, saturin_mir.shape[0])
    sample_indices = np.random.choice(saturin_mir.shape[0], n_samples_plot, replace=False)
    
    for idx in sample_indices:
        axes[0].plot(saturin_mir.columns, saturin_mir.iloc[idx], alpha=0.5, linewidth=0.8)
    
    axes[0].set_xlabel('Wavenumber (cm⁻¹)', fontsize=11)
    axes[0].set_ylabel('Reflectance', fontsize=11)
    axes[0].set_title('Raw MIR Spectral Curves (n=50 random samples)', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')
    axes[0].grid(True, alpha=0.3)
    
    # Plot 1B: Smoothed MIR spectra
    for idx in sample_indices:
        axes[1].plot(saturin_mir_smooth.columns, saturin_mir_smooth.iloc[idx], alpha=0.5, linewidth=0.8)
    
    axes[1].set_xlabel('Wavenumber (cm⁻¹)', fontsize=11)
    axes[1].set_ylabel('Reflectance (smoothed)', fontsize=11)
    axes[1].set_title('Smoothed MIR Spectral Curves (Savitzky-Golay filter)', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')
    axes[1].grid(True, alpha=0.3)
    
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig('spectroscopy_01_spectral_curves.png', dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
    plt.show()
    
    print("\n✓ Plot saved: spectroscopy_01_spectral_curves.png")

# ============================================================================
# PLOT 2: PCA VARIANCE EXPLAINED (Scree Plot)
# ============================================================================
print("\n" + "=" * 80)
print("PLOT 2: PCA VARIANCE EXPLAINED - SCREE PLOT")
print("=" * 80)

if saturin_mir_pca is not None and explained_var_mir is not None:
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(14, 5))
    
    # Scree plot - Individual variance
    axes[0].bar(range(1, len(explained_var_mir) + 1), explained_var_mir * 100, 
                alpha=0.7, color='steelblue', edgecolor='black', linewidth=1.5)
    axes[0].set_xlabel('Principal Component', fontsize=11)
    axes[0].set_ylabel('Variance Explained (%)', fontsize=11)
    axes[0].set_title('Individual Variance Explained by Each PC', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')
    axes[0].grid(True, alpha=0.3, axis='y')
    
    # Cumulative variance
    cumvar = np.cumsum(explained_var_mir) * 100
    axes[1].plot(range(1, len(cumvar) + 1), cumvar, 'o-', linewidth=2, 
                 markersize=8, color='darkgreen', label='Cumulative Variance')
    axes[1].axhline(y=80, color='r', linestyle='--', linewidth=2, label='80% threshold')
    axes[1].axhline(y=90, color='orange', linestyle='--', linewidth=2, label='90% threshold')
    axes[1].fill_between(range(1, len(cumvar) + 1), cumvar, alpha=0.3, color='darkgreen')
    axes[1].set_xlabel('Principal Component', fontsize=11)
    axes[1].set_ylabel('Cumulative Variance Explained (%)', fontsize=11)
    axes[1].set_title('Cumulative Variance Explained', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')
    axes[1].legend(fontsize=10)
    axes[1].grid(True, alpha=0.3)
    
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig('spectroscopy_02_pca_variance.png', dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
    plt.show()
    
    print("\n✓ Plot saved: spectroscopy_02_pca_variance.png")
    print(f"  Variance explained by first 3 PCs: {cumvar[2]:.2f}%")

# ============================================================================
# PLOT 3: PCA SCORE PLOT (PC1 vs PC2)
# ============================================================================
print("\n" + "=" * 80)
print("PLOT 3: PCA SCORE PLOT - PC1 vs PC2")
print("=" * 80)

if saturin_mir_pca is not None:
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 8))
    
    # Scatter plot of PC1 vs PC2
    scatter = ax.scatter(saturin_mir_pca[:, 0], saturin_mir_pca[:, 1], 
                        c=saturin_mir_pca[:, 0], cmap='coolwarm', 
                        alpha=0.6, s=50, edgecolor='black', linewidth=0.5)
    
    ax.set_xlabel(f'PC1 ({explained_var_mir[0]*100:.2f}% variance)', fontsize=11)
    ax.set_ylabel(f'PC2 ({explained_var_mir[1]*100:.2f}% variance)', fontsize=11)
    ax.set_title('PCA Score Plot: PC1 vs PC2 (MIR Data)', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')
    ax.grid(True, alpha=0.3)
    ax.axhline(y=0, color='k', linestyle='-', linewidth=0.5)
    ax.axvline(x=0, color='k', linestyle='-', linewidth=0.5)
    
    cbar = plt.colorbar(scatter, ax=ax)
    cbar.set_label('PC1 Score', fontsize=10)
    
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig('spectroscopy_03_pca_score_plot.png', dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
    plt.show()
    
    print("\n✓ Plot saved: spectroscopy_03_pca_score_plot.png")

# ============================================================================
# PLOT 4: SPECTRAL INDICES DISTRIBUTION
# ============================================================================
print("\n" + "=" * 80)
print("PLOT 4: SPECTRAL INDICES DISTRIBUTION")
print("=" * 80)

if mir_indices is not None:
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(14, 10))
    
    indices_to_plot = list(mir_indices.columns)[:4]
    
    for idx, (ax, col) in enumerate(zip(axes.flat, indices_to_plot)):
        # Histogram
        ax.hist(mir_indices[col], bins=50, alpha=0.7, color='skyblue', edgecolor='black')
        ax.set_xlabel(col, fontsize=10)
        ax.set_ylabel('Frequency', fontsize=10)
        ax.set_title(f'Distribution: {col}', fontsize=11, fontweight='bold')
        ax.grid(True, alpha=0.3, axis='y')
        
        # Add statistics
        mean_val = mir_indices[col].mean()
        std_val = mir_indices[col].std()
        ax.axvline(mean_val, color='red', linestyle='--', linewidth=2, label=f'Mean: {mean_val:.3f}')
        ax.axvline(mean_val - std_val, color='orange', linestyle=':', linewidth=1.5, label=f'±1σ: {std_val:.3f}')
        ax.axvline(mean_val + std_val, color='orange', linestyle=':', linewidth=1.5)
        ax.legend(fontsize=9)
    
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig('spectroscopy_04_spectral_indices_dist.png', dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
    plt.show()
    
    print("\n✓ Plot saved: spectroscopy_04_spectral_indices_dist.png")

# ============================================================================
# PLOT 5: SPECTRAL HEATMAP
# ============================================================================
print("\n" + "=" * 80)
print("PLOT 5: SPECTRAL HEATMAP (Subset of Samples)")
print("=" * 80)

if saturin_mir_scaled is not None:
    # Select subset of samples and spectral bands for visualization
    n_samples_heat = min(100, saturin_mir_scaled.shape[0])
    n_bands = min(100, saturin_mir_scaled.shape[1])
    
    heatmap_data = saturin_mir_scaled.iloc[:n_samples_heat, ::saturin_mir_scaled.shape[1]//n_bands]
    
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(14, 8))
    
    sns.heatmap(heatmap_data, cmap='RdYlBu_r', cbar_kws={'label': 'Standardized Reflectance'},
                ax=ax, yticklabels=False, xticklabels=False)
    
    ax.set_xlabel('Spectral Bands (sampled)', fontsize=11)
    ax.set_ylabel(f'Samples (n={n_samples_heat})', fontsize=11)
    ax.set_title('MIR Spectral Data Heatmap (Standardized)', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')
    
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig('spectroscopy_05_spectral_heatmap.png', dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
    plt.show()
    
    print("\n✓ Plot saved: spectroscopy_05_spectral_heatmap.png")

# ============================================================================
# PLOT 6: NOISE ANALYSIS
# ============================================================================
print("\n" + "=" * 80)
print("PLOT 6: NOISE ANALYSIS - SPECTRAL STANDARD DEVIATION")
print("=" * 80)

if spectral_std_mir is not None:
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(14, 5))
    
    # Histogram of spectral std
    axes[0].hist(spectral_std_mir, bins=50, alpha=0.7, color='lightcoral', edgecolor='black')
    axes[0].axvline(threshold_high_mir, color='red', linestyle='--', linewidth=2, 
                    label=f'95th percentile: {threshold_high_mir:.4f}')
    axes[0].set_xlabel('Spectral Std Dev', fontsize=11)
    axes[0].set_ylabel('Frequency', fontsize=11)
    axes[0].set_title('Distribution of Spectral Noise (Std Dev)', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')
    axes[0].legend(fontsize=10)
    axes[0].grid(True, alpha=0.3, axis='y')
    
    # Time series of noise
    axes[1].plot(range(len(spectral_std_mir)), spectral_std_mir.values, 'o-', 
                 alpha=0.6, markersize=4, color='darkred', linewidth=1)
    axes[1].axhline(threshold_high_mir, color='red', linestyle='--', linewidth=2, 
                    label=f'Noise threshold')
    axes[1].fill_between(range(len(spectral_std_mir)), threshold_high_mir, 
                         spectral_std_mir.values.max(), alpha=0.2, color='red', label='High-noise region')
    axes[1].set_xlabel('Sample Index', fontsize=11)
    axes[1].set_ylabel('Spectral Std Dev', fontsize=11)
    axes[1].set_title('Spectral Noise Level by Sample', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')
    axes[1].legend(fontsize=10)
    axes[1].grid(True, alpha=0.3)
    
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig('spectroscopy_06_noise_analysis.png', dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
    plt.show()
    
    print(f"\n✓ Plot saved: spectroscopy_06_noise_analysis.png")
    print(f"  Noisy samples (>threshold): {len(noisy_mir)}/{len(spectral_std_mir)}")

# ============================================================================
# PLOT 7: MEAN SPECTRAL PROFILE
# ============================================================================
print("\n" + "=" * 80)
print("PLOT 7: MEAN SPECTRAL PROFILE WITH ERROR BANDS")
print("=" * 80)

if saturin_mir is not None:
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(14, 6))
    
    # Calculate mean and std for each band
    mean_spectrum = saturin_mir.mean(axis=0)
    std_spectrum = saturin_mir.std(axis=0)
    
    # Plot mean with confidence band
    ax.plot(saturin_mir.columns, mean_spectrum, color='darkblue', linewidth=2.5, label='Mean spectrum')
    ax.fill_between(saturin_mir.columns, 
                     mean_spectrum - std_spectrum, 
                     mean_spectrum + std_spectrum,
                     alpha=0.3, color='lightblue', label='±1 Std Dev')
    ax.fill_between(saturin_mir.columns, 
                     mean_spectrum - 2*std_spectrum, 
                     mean_spectrum + 2*std_spectrum,
                     alpha=0.15, color='lightblue', label='±2 Std Dev')
    
    ax.set_xlabel('Wavenumber (cm⁻¹)', fontsize=11)
    ax.set_ylabel('Reflectance', fontsize=11)
    ax.set_title('Mean MIR Spectral Profile with Confidence Bands', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')
    ax.legend(fontsize=10, loc='upper right')
    ax.grid(True, alpha=0.3)
    
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig('spectroscopy_07_mean_spectrum.png', dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
    plt.show()
    
    print("\n✓ Plot saved: spectroscopy_07_mean_spectrum.png")

# ============================================================================
# SUMMARY
# ============================================================================
print("\n" + "=" * 80)
print("VISUALIZATION COMPLETE")
